## 🤔 Seedance 2.0 とは？

**Seedance 2.0** は**ByteDance**が開発した動画生成モデルで、業界初の**4モーダル同時入力**をサポートするモデルです — 画像、動画、音声、テキスト。

**Key Features:**
- 🎥 **テキストから動画** — テキスト説明から動画を生成
- 🖼️ **画像から動画** — 静止画をダイナミックな動画にアニメーション化
- 📹 **動画から動画** — 既存の動画を変換・拡張
- 🎵 **音声駆動** — 音声入力で動画を生成
- 📐 **最大1080p解像度**、4〜15秒の長さ
- 🔊 **自動吹き替え＆スコアリング** — 自動ナレーションとBGM

### No. 1: Seedance 2.0：15秒で描く、映画のような日本のロマンス短編映画

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 向けの、非常に詳細な 15 秒間のマルチシーンプロンプト。映画のような超リアルな日本の高校生の純愛短編映画を生成するように設計されています。プロンプトは、シーン設定（誰もいない教室、暖かく金色に輝く太陽の光、舞い上がる埃）、カメラの動き、キャラクターの一貫性（変形やずれがないこと）、繊細な微表情、同期した呼吸と唇の動き、セリフ、効果音（蝉の声、ペンが紙を擦る音、低周波の心臓の鼓動、柔らかなピアノの音）を指定しています。物語は、書いている少女と、彼女を密かに見つめる少年との間の、強烈でぎこちなく、親密な感情的緊張に焦点を当て、最終的には内気な対決へと発展します。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
15秒の映画のような日本のドラマ、純愛の曖昧な短編映画、超リアルな画質、午後の人気のない教室に差し込む温かい金色の太陽の光がブラインドを通して並んだ机に降り注ぎ、光の筋の中を細かな埃がゆっくりと舞い上がる。古い木製の机、極めて自然で繊細な動き、呼吸、目の緊張感。登場人物の顔、服装、髪型は変形、ずれ、アーティファクトなく一貫して維持され、呼吸に合わせて胸がわずかに上下する様子もリアル。浅い被写界深度、クリーミーにぼやけた背景、温かいフィルムグレイン、8Kのシャープさ、日本の青春の抑えられた胸キュンするような息苦しい雰囲気。
0-4秒：デスクトップのミディアムショットから、並んで座る二人の横顔のクローズアップへと、極めてゆっくりとプッシュインショット。夏服の制服を着た純粋な少女がうつむいてノートに集中して書き込んでおり、長い黒髪と耳元の後れ毛が微風に優しくなびく。長いまつげが繊細な影を落とし、肌は自然なピンク色で柔らかく、集中しているために無意識に口角がわずかに上がっている。呼吸は軽く均一。
4-9秒：少年のクローズアップに切り替わる。制服の襟はわずかに緩んでおり、彼は机に肘をついて、こっそりと顔を向けて彼女を見つめている。彼の瞳には優しく抑えられた愛情と優しさが満ちており、瞳孔はわずかに開いている。喉仏が優しく上下する。突然、彼女のペンが止まったことに気づき、慌てて自分のノートを見るふりをして顔をそらす。耳たぶはすぐにわずかに赤くなり、ペンを握る指先はわずかに震えている。前髪の間から時折彼女をちらりと見やり、呼吸はわずかに乱れ、落ち着こうと唇をきつく閉じている。
9-15秒：同じフレームに二人の顔の超クローズアップ、スローモーションで突然目が合う：少女はゆっくりと顔を向け、最初はぼんやりとした驚きの表情を見せるが、すぐに恥ずかしそうに0.3秒間うつむき、下唇を優しく噛む。頬と耳たぶは瞬時に桜色に染まり、潤んだまつげがはにかむように再び彼を見上げ、柔らかく恥ずかしそうに「…何見てるの？」とささやく。少年は完全に固まり、瞳孔が開き、0.4秒間呆然とした後、慌てて静かにどもりながら「な、なんでもない…」と答える。少女はさらに小さな声で、唇を噛みながら再び彼をちらりと見て、ささやき続ける。「…嘘つき。」少年は一瞬の間を置き、それから優しくため息をつき、ささやく。「…君を見てただけだよ。」彼の口角はゆっくりと上がり、はにかむような、優しい、少し歪んだ笑顔を見せる。目尻には細い線が現れ、呼吸は明らかに深くなる。見えない電流が二人の顔の間の曖昧な緊張を引き寄せ、互いの呼吸の温度を分かち合う。背景はクリーミーで夢のような光の斑点、温かいハロー、そして微細な空気の粒子が幾重にも重なって完全に溶け込んでいる。
リップシンクは自然で正確、感情的な微細な震えと呼吸は同期しており、セリフは恥ずかしそうなトーンの低エネルギーなささやき声で、200-400ミリ秒の自然な短い間があり、話すときに口はわずかに動くだけで、誇張やロボットのような不自然さはなく、完璧な自然なリップシンクと感情の信憑性。
全体的な効果音：遠くでかすかに聞こえる夏のセミの鳴き声、ペンが紙に触れる柔らかい擦れる音、ほとんど聞こえない心臓の低周波の鼓動、最後に非常に軽やかで風通しの良いピアノの音にフェードアウトする。セリフはささやき声として完全に自然にシーンに溶け込んでおり、少女の声は柔らかく恥ずかしがり屋で、少年の声は慌てたどもりから優しい声へと変化する。
キャラクターのアイデンティティは一貫して維持され、リアルで繊細な頭の傾き、目の動き、呼吸の同期があり、テキスト、透かし、字幕はなく、純粋な日本スタイルの青春の秘めた恋の胸キュンサスペンス。
```

### No. 2: ハリウッド オートクチュール ファンタジー ビデオ プロンプト

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 向けの、ハリウッドのオートクチュールファンタジー映画を制作するためにデザインされた、詳細かつ複数シーンにわたる動画生成プロンプト。このプロンプトは、スタイル、解像度 (8K)、レンダリングエンジン (Unreal Engine 5)、再生時間 (15 秒) を指定し、液体状の青と白の磁器をまとったモデルが、飛び散って水墨画のツバメになり、最終的に 3D の流体インクの渦を形成するという、3 つの異なるカメラ/アクションシーケンスを含みます。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
[スタイル] ハリウッドのオートクチュール ファンタジー超大作、8K 超高精細、フォトリアリスティック、ハイファッション エディトリアル スタイル、Unreal Engine 5 の流体レンダリング、視覚的錯覚。[尺] 15 秒。[シーン] 果てしなく広がる、現実のウユニ塩湖（スカイミラー）の塩原。空には重苦しい暗い雲が立ち込め、地面はすべてを鏡のように完璧に映し出し、全体的にミニマリストでクールなトーンの絵が広がる。[00:00-00:05] ショット 1: オートクチュールの登場と陶器のような肌。カメラ位置: 極端なローアングルからの上向きショット、超望遠レンズでズームイン。アクション: 非常に特徴的なハイファッションの顔を持つアジア人女性モデルが、水面をクールに歩く。効果: 彼女は布ではなく、流れるような本物の液体青白磁でできたロングドレスを身につけている。歩くたびに、スカートは本物の陶器のような澄んだ衝突音を立て、表面には流れるような光沢がある。伝統的な青と白の模様が、白い陶器のような質感のスカートの上を生きているかのように動き回る。[00:05-00:10] ショット 2: 物理的な粉砕と水墨画のような降下。カメラ位置: 顔の極端なクローズアップ、フォーカスが急速に引き戻される。アクション: モデルが突然立ち止まり、冷たくカメラを見つめ、指をパチンと鳴らす。効果: 指を鳴らした瞬間、彼女の青白磁のドレスは落ちることなく、瞬時に何千もの極めてフォトリアリスティックな水墨画のツバメに爆発する。これらのツバメは本物の水滴と墨の跡を運び、空気中に黒い流体の残像を引きずりながら、彼女の周りを狂ったように旋回する。[00:10-00:15] ショット 3: 次元的な溶解と深淵の反射。カメラ位置: 高高度からのオーバーヘッドショット、カメラが急速に回転し下降する。アクション: 水墨画のツバメの群れが、モデルの足元の鏡のような湖水に飛び込む。効果: 元々固かった塩湖の表面張力が瞬時に消滅する。極めてリアルな世界全体が、澄んだ水に濃い墨を落としたように激しく滲み、溶解し始める。現実の暗い雲とモデルの姿は、極めて壮大な 3D 流体墨渦に完全に変容し、カメラを白と黒が織りなす深淵へと完全に飲み込む。
```

### No. 3: 現代の田舎の美学が癒しをもたらす短編映像プロンプト

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 用の、現代的な田園美学スタイルで癒しと映画のような短編フィルムを生成するための、詳細な 3 ショットプロンプトです。スタイル（シネマティックコマーシャル、4K/8K、エクストリームマクロ、自然光、ASMR）、設定（庭の見えるモダンなオープンキッチン）、登場人物（リネンを身につけた集中力のあるクリエイター）、そして 3 つのシーンでの具体的なアクション（トマトを摘む、正確にカットする、静かに食べる瞬間）が指定されています。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
[スタイル]
モダンな田舎の美学、映画のようなコマーシャル品質、Sony A7S3/シネマカメラで撮影、4K/8K の超クリアな画質、エクストリームマクロ、自然で透明感のあるライティング、癒しの ASMR、時代劇のような雰囲気はなし。

[シーン]
手入れの行き届いたモダンな農家のオープンキッチン、背景には青々とした菜園、明るい日差し。

[登場人物]
モダンな田舎のクリエイター、黒い長い髪を木製のヘアピンで無造作にまとめ、ダークブルーの快適なリネン服を着用、クリアなメイク、集中した穏やかな瞳。

[ショット詳細]
[00:00-00:05] ショット 1：朝の収穫（新鮮さ）
映像：高精細なクローズアップ。朝の太陽が植物にサイドバックライトを当てる。
動作：クリエイターの素手（長く清潔な指）が、つるから露の輝く真っ赤なトマトを摘み取る。
詳細：非常にシャープなフォーカスで、トマトの表面の産毛と水滴が滑り落ちる軌跡がはっきりと見える。背景はぼかされた高品質の緑。

[00:05-00:10] ショット 2：究極の職人技（匠の技）
映像：屋内のストーブエリア、生活感がありながらも清潔。
動作：クリエイターが野菜を切る、動きは熟練していて正確（パフォーマンスではない）。
詳細：マクロレンズが、包丁の刃が食材を切り裂き、汁が飛び散る瞬間を捉える。その後、土鍋のストーブでオレンジ色の炎が揺らめく様子に切り替わり、光と影は暖かくリアル。

[00:10-00:15] ショット 3：穏やかな時間（その瞬間）
映像：フルショット/ミディアムショット。
動作：庭の木製の長いテーブルに繊細な家庭料理が置かれる。クリエイターは静かに座り、はみ出した髪をそっと整え、一口食べる。
雰囲気：逆光の中で湯気がゆっくりと立ち上り、風の音さえ聞こえそうなほど静かなシーンは、現代人が切望する究極のリラックス感を表現している。
```

### No. 4: Demon Slayer ライブアクションバトルプロンプト for Seedance 2.0

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 用の、詳細で高エネルギーな動画プロンプト。鬼滅の刃風のバトル（水の呼吸 vs 雷の呼吸）を実写で15秒間にわたって描く。プロンプトでは、スタイル（ハリウッド実写漫画化、ダークサムライ、4K、極端なクイックカット、パーティクルライトエフェクト）、設定（夜の霧深い森）、そしてキャラクターのアクション、パワーアップシーケンス、最終的な激突を詳述する3つの異なるショットが指定されている。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
実写漫画化 · 呼吸法決戦（15秒 · 超絶エフェクト版）
【核心的焦点】：水の呼吸（青い水龍）VS 雷の呼吸（黄金の稲妻）、実写による超高速デュエル。
【スタイル】：ハリウッド実写漫画化映画のクオリティ、ダークな侍スタイル、4K 超高画質、超高速カット、パーティクル光エフェクト爆発、ゴア表現なし。
【尺】：15秒
【シーン】：月明かりの霧深い森、ぬかるんだ地面、落ち葉。
[00:00-00:05] ショット 1：水ノ旋律 · 序ノ型（チャージ感）
ビジュアル：緑と黒の市松模様の羽織をまとった若い侍が、月明かりの下で重心を低くし、両手で刀を構える。
アクション：彼が深く息を吸い込むと、周囲の空気が瞬時に凝固する。刀を抜くと同時に、高圧の水流が凝縮された巨大な青い水龍が空中に現れ、彼の体と刀の周りを高速で回転し、水が流れる轟音を発する。
特殊効果の詳細：水流にはリアルな水しぶきの感覚があり、暗い森を照らす。
[00:05-00:10] ショット 2：雷ノ閃光 · 進撃（超高速感）
ビジュアル：向かい合う敵、黄色の三角模様の羽織をまとった金髪の剣士が、極端に体を低くし、居合術の構えを取る。
アクション：地面が突然爆発する。彼の全身は瞬時にまばゆい黄金の稲妻の残像と化し、肉眼では見えない速度で木々の間を「Z」字型に屈折しながら高速で進撃する。
特殊効果の詳細：彼が通過した場所には、黄金の電弧と焦げた落ち葉が残る。
[00:10-00:15] ショット 3：水雷激突 · 終ノ響（奥義の衝突）
ビジュアル：超高速の正面衝突。若い侍は巨大な青い水龍を振り下ろして攻撃を迎え撃ち、稲妻と化した金髪の剣士がそれに激突する。
アクション：フレーム中央で二本の刀が激しく衝突する。
特殊効果のスペクタクル：青い水龍と黄金の稲妻が瞬時に爆発し、巨大な水雷エネルギーの嵐となって外側へ広がる。周囲の大木はエネルギー波によって真っ二つに折れ、泥、水、光がカメラを覆い隠す。シーンは極めてまばゆい青、黄、白の光の中で終わる。
```

### No. 5: Seedance 2.0: 80歳のラッパー MV

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 用の、80 歳の女性をフィーチャーした 16:9 の横長ストリートラップミュージックビデオ (MV) を生成するための詳細な 15 秒プロンプト。プロンプトでは、スタイル (ネオンパープル/ブルーのクールトーン、爆発的な雰囲気)、キャラクターの外見 (銀髪、レザージャケット、ヒップホップアクセサリー)、シーンの内訳 (0～3 秒のイントロ、3～7 秒のラップ、7～11 秒のダンス、11～15 秒のクライマックス/結論)、特定のラップ歌詞、カメラテクニック (ローアングル、360 度回転、クイックカット)、サウンドデザイン (トラップエレクトロニックミュージック、重厚な 808 ドラム) が指定されています。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
16:9 の横長スクリーン、ストリートラップの MV スタイル、ネオンパープルとブルーのクールトーン、爆発的でクールかつ激しい雰囲気。0-3 秒: ミディアムショットのプッシュイン、ネオンが点滅する都会の夜景、グラフィティウォールの前に立つ 80 歳の白髪の女性。短く銀白色の髪はきっちりとしたスリックバック、はっきりとした四角い顔の輪郭、こめかみに向かって傾斜した剣のような眉、電光石火のような鋭い目、目尻のしわは時の勲章のよう、口元には自信に満ちた笑み。白いプリント T シャツ (胸に大きな黒文字で「YOLO」) の上に黒いレザージャケット、黒いカーゴパンツ、白いハイカットスニーカーを着用。首には太い金のネックレス、手首にはシルバーのブレスレット。両手でマイクを掲げ、BGM の力強いドラムビートが始まり、老婆の目は鋭くなり、唇が開いてラップを開始。3-7 秒: ミディアムショット + クローズアップの切り替え、老婆がラップを始める。非常に強いリズム感で、頭を振る動きに合わせて銀髪が舞い、片手でマイクを持ち、もう一方の手はリズムに合わせてジェスチャーをする。人差し指をカメラに向けたり、手のひらでリズムを上下に刻んだり、ヒップホップのジェスチャーをしたり、動きは滑らかで流れるよう。目は鋭くカメラを直視し、表情に合わせてしわが鮮やかに動き、唇が素早く開閉して歌詞を吐き出す: [ラップ歌詞] 「80 歳の足でも、お前より高く跳べるぜ！銀髪がなびく、これが私の誇り！年寄りなんて言うな、私のフローはお前より上だ、お前がラップしてた頃、私はディスコを聴いてたんだ！」(高速、強いリズム、激しい態度) クイックカット: 顔のクローズアップ、手の動き、全身の揺れ、横顔のシルエット、BGM のビートに同期。7-11 秒: ダンスセグメント、カメラが引いて全身を映し出す。老婆がダンスを始める。最初はクラシックなヒップホップバウンス、次にきれいなストリートダンスのフリーズ、続いて肩からつま先まで伝わるボディウェーブ、そして素早いフットワーク。動きはクリーンでシャープ、ネオンライトの下で銀髪が舞い、レザージャケットが空中でひらめく。踊りながらラップを続ける: [ラップ歌詞] 「足腰は軽やか、スピードは衰えない、私の歌詞は時に刻まれる！お前はスマホで遊ぶ、私はビートで遊ぶ、80 年の人生、このバースに書き込む！」(より速いリズム、より強いトーン) ローアングルからのアッパーショット + 360 度囲むショットで、老婆のクールで激しいダンスムーブを捉える。11-15 秒: クライマックスのエンディング、老婆がクールにターンし、銀髪が空中に弧を描く。カメラに向かって指で「シーッ」というジェスチャーをし、その後、唇をマイクに近づけ、低く魅力的な声で最後のセリフを歌う: [リアリティ歌詞] 「時は決して美しさを打ち負かさない、私はただ若さを経験する方法を変えただけ…」(遅いリズム、深い感情、余韻を残す終わり方) カメラがゆっくりと老婆の目のクローズアップにプッシュインする。目尻のしわはすべて物語を語り、その視線は依然として鋭いが、優しさも帯びている。BGM はクライマックスで突然停止し、老婆のクールでありながら少し優しい笑顔でフレームがフリーズする。ビネット + ネオンパープルの光のハロー。
```

### No. 6: シネマティックなストリートレース映像生成プロンプト

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 説明

Seedance 2.0 を使用して、夜のシネマティックなストリートレース映像を生成するための詳細なプロンプト。カメラワーク、ショットのタイミング（0〜12秒）、環境の詳細、および視覚スタイル（『ワイルド・スピード』風）を指定しています。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
夜のシネマティックなストリートレース、高性能な車内でハンドルを握るドライバーの集中した表情、鋭い眼差し、フロントガラスに反射する街の明かり、急加速直前の高まる緊張感

カメラ：シームレスなトランジションを伴う高速マルチアングルシステム、車内クローズアップ → 肩越しショット → 車外トラッキング → ローアングルショット、超ダイナミックなカメラワーク、ウィップパン ＋ スピードランプ・トランジション ＋ モーションブラーによるカットのマスキング、連続的な流れの演出

(0-2秒) ドライバーの車内クローズアップ、シフトレバーを握り締める手、かすかな呼吸、ダッシュボードの光
(2-4秒) 肩越しショット、ネオン輝く街へと続く道路、高まるエンジンの振動
(4-6秒) NOS ボタンを押す指の超クローズアップ、点火の瞬間
(6-8秒) 爆発的な加速、カメラが車外サイドトラッキングショットに切り替わり、猛烈なスピードで車が前方に飛び出す
(8-10秒) アスファルトに近い超ローアングルショット、猛回転するホイール、背後に流れる景色
(10-12秒) 狭い通りでの高速チェイス、急カーブ、アングル間を切り替えるウィップパン、スピード感を強調する反射と光の軌跡

高密度な夜の都市環境、ネオンを反射する濡れたアスファルト、トンネルの通過、流れる街灯、高速都市の雰囲気
超リアル、『ワイルド・スピード』にインスパイアされたエネルギー、フォトリアルなライティング、強烈なモーションブラー、高コントラストなネオンの反射、シネマティックな被写界深度、極限のスピード感、流れるようなトランジション、歪みや引き延ばしなし
```

#### 📝 プロンプト

### Realistic Korean Man Park Vlog

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A highly detailed video generation prompt designed to create a realistic UGC vlog featuring a young Korean man in a public park, including storyboard instructions and specific camera effects like handheld shaking.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
CAMERA:
DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld footage feel. Point of view of JAY holding the camera directly in hand. Maintain hand shake, misaligned framing, delayed focus pull, clumsy zoom in/out, occasional self-cam framing where the face gets cut off, and imperfect shots that lose the subject. Every shot is JAY holding the camcorder himself, filming in selfie-cam or first-person style. The camcorder itself never appears on screen.

LOOK:
DV 16mm tape camcorder footage look. Soft, slightly blurry digital tape quality, faint tape noise, highlights that bloom subtly in bright daylight, gentle auto-exposure shifts as the camera moves between sunlight and shade, muted color contrast, realistic skin tones.

STYLE:
Relaxed daytime vlog with a calm, genuine, low-energy-but-happy vibe. Slightly tired after a long walk or outdoor workout, but satisfied and enjoying the fresh air. Handheld throughout, natural pauses between lines, occasional quiet breathing, authentic unposed expressions, documentary realism.

Character

JAY — a handsome Korean man in his 20s. Thick black hair slightly tousled from being outdoors, sharp yet warm Korean facial features, healthy glowing skin with a light sheen of sweat, expressive eyes, athletic slim build. Wearing a modest long-sleeve athletic top or lightweight hoodie, loose-fit joggers or athletic pants, comfortable sneakers. Fully covered arms and torso. No jewelry.

Setting

A peaceful public park during the daytime under natural sunlight. Wide green lawns, mature trees casting soft shadows, paved walking paths, park benches, trimmed bushes, birds occasionally flying overhead, gentle breeze moving the leaves, distant people walking or jogging naturally in the background without drawing attention, bright blue sky with soft clouds. Everything feels calm, open, and naturally lit.

Storyboard

(~2s, camera propped against a park bench, medium shot)
He walks into frame breathing slightly heavier from a walk or light workout, wipes his forehead with the back of his hand, gives a small relaxed smile.

JAY:
""Okay... just finished my walk, such a nice day.""

(~2s, handheld, slow pan across the park)
Camera slowly drifts across the green trees, walking paths, and open lawn before returning to him.

JAY (off-screen, softly):
""It's so peaceful here today.""

(~2s, medium handheld, beside a park bench)
He grabs his water bottle from the bench, takes a long drink, then exhales comfortably afterward.

JAY:
""Needed that so bad.""

(~1.5s, macro insert, shallow DOF)
Close-up on his hand wiping condensation from the water bottle, sunlight reflecting off tiny water droplets. No dialogue—only soft breathing and subtle ambient park sounds.

(~2s, medium handheld, open grassy area)
He props the camera against the bench, steps back into frame, casually stretches, then playfully does a few quick dance moves or light footwork before laughing
```

### Pixar Style Pigeon Chase Animation

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A long-form episodic video prompt for a Pixar-inspired 3D animated short film about a man and his pigeon nemesis.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
MY NEMESIS IS A PIGEON” Style: Pixar-Inspired 3D Animation • Comedy • Family-Friendly • Viral Short Film ⸻ 🚪 ACT 1 — THE DAILY FEAR Dave is getting ready to leave his apartment. But something feels wrong. Before stepping outside, he carefully checks every direction. Left. Right. The sky. Behind a nearby tree. Even under a parked car. He’s clearly done this many times before. The audience immediately wonders: “What is this guy afraid of?” Dave finally relaxes. Maybe today will be different. ⸻ 🐦 ACT 2 — THE ENEMY RETURNS The moment Dave steps outside, everything changes. A shadow slowly moves across his face. He looks up. Perched high above him sits an angry pigeon. Not an ordinary pigeon. This pigeon looks like a movie villain. It stares directly at Dave. Waiting. Watching. The audience instantly understands: These two have history. ⸻ 🏃 ACT 3 — THE CHASE Without warning, the pigeon launches. Dave immediately panics. The chase begins. He runs through the city. Dodging pedestrians. Jumping around benches. Desperately trying to escape. The pigeon follows every move perfectly. No matter what Dave does, the pigeon always seems one step ahead. The audience starts laughing because the pigeon behaves less like a bird and more like a supervillain. ⸻ 🚌 ACT 4 — FALSE HOPE Dave spots a bus and jumps onboard. Finally safe. He collapses into a seat. Relieved. Then he hears a familiar sound. Coo. The pigeon is already inside the bus. Dave completely loses it. He screams for help. Begs the driver to stop. The bus screeches to a halt. Dave jumps out and runs. The audience realizes there is absolutely no escaping this bird. ⸻ 🌳 ACT 5 — THE PARK Dave runs into a nearby park. Exhausted. Breathing heavily. He hides behind a giant statue. Silence. No flapping. No cooing. Nothing. For the first time all day, he believes he has finally escaped. The danger seems over. ⸻ 🍟 ACT 6 — THE REVEAL A single french fry suddenly falls from the sky. Landing directly at Dave’s feet. His face drops instantly. He already knows what it means. Slowly, he looks up. The pigeon stands triumphantly on top of the statue. Then another french fry falls. As it drops— The story flashes back in black and white. Months earlier. A tiny baby pigeon excitedly approaches its favorite french fry. Just before it reaches the snack— Dave casually grabs it and eats it. The baby pigeon watches in absolute heartbreak. The audience finally discovers the truth. This entire feud started because of one stolen french fry. ⸻ 💩 ACT 7 — REVENGE COMPLETE Back in the present. Dave stares at the pigeon in disbelief. After all this time… The pigeon is still angry. The pigeon slowly nods. Then delivers one final perfectly aimed attack. Direct hit. Dave stands frozen in defeat. The pigeon proudly celebrates from atop the statue. Cut to black.
```

### Topview Film Studio、実際に使ってみた... これはかなり使い勝手良いですね！✨


![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
アニメーションのアクションシーン、美しいドレスを着た美女と人型ロボットが、誰もいない舞踏会のホール内で激しく銃撃戦を繰り広げる大迫力のシーンとなる60秒のショートフィルムを生成してください。
魅力的なキャラクター、表現力のある映像、人型ロボットはホーミング銃弾を発射すると、その銃弾は噴煙航跡を引きながら美女を追尾する。
美女はホール内をパルクールのようなアクションで縦横無尽に三次元的な動きで回避行動を取りつつ、ときおり拳銃でロボットを射撃する。
カメラアングルとカメラワークは美女と人型ロボットを魅力的に演出するよう、派手な3D旋回系の動作で撮影を続ける。

The smartphone camera briefly struggles to focus through thick smoke and floating ash particles, auto-adjusting its exposure to the bright pyrotechnic flashes. Natural production audio only: loud hydraulic hiss, pyrotechnic pops, shattering plaster, studio intercom chatter, warehouse ventilation hum, crew reaction gasps.

### Urban Battlefield Action Scene

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> An explosive, high-tension boss fight sequence between a techwear heroine and a heavy axe-wielding clown in an industrial ruin.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
Create a 15-second ultra-realistic cinematic action scene in strict 16:9 landscape, native 4K, 24fps. Tone: explosive, dangerous, fast, high-tension, and visually thrilling. This is Scene 1 of a two-part boss-fight-style action sequence and must end on a cliffhanger that naturally leads into Scene 2. REFERENCE ROLES: image1 = Pyona, the main heroine. Use image1 as the strict reference for Pyona’s face, facial proportions, light pastel-blue bob hairstyle, hair color, white cloud hair clip, blue fish hair clip, body proportions, age impression, exact outfit, styling, and overall recognizable identity. Preserve her exact cropped urban-techwear action outfit throughout: light gray cropped hooded jacket, clearly visible white crop-top feel underneath, black asymmetrical utility mini-skirt / short-bottom styling, black utility pouch or crossbody detail, black socks, and black combat boots. Do not redesign her. image2 = the clown enemy. Use image2 as the strict reference for the clown’s identity, proportions, terrifying costume, giant heavy axe, and overall menacing appearance. Preserve the same clown and same weapon consistently throughout. CORE ACTION RULE: Pyona must never block the axe blade with her bare body. She survives and fights through prediction, timing, footwork, body-angle control, narrow dodges, aerial repositioning, spacing, and fast counters. The clown must feel physically overwhelming, heavy, and destructive. Pyona stays alive and gains control through speed and precision, not brute force. ENVIRONMENT: A ruined urban battlefield at night, like a collapsed industrial ruin or destroyed concrete structure. Broken walls, exposed rebar, cracked ground, rubble piles, dust clouds, shattered concrete, and damaged surfaces everywhere. The axe attacks must visibly damage the environment: scraping the ground, throwing sparks and debris, cracking walls, carving trenches, and blasting rubble outward. Maintain clear spatial consistency. STYLE: Live-action photorealism with strong action readability. High-end cinematic fight energy, like a boss fight in a dark action film. Realistic body weight, believable recoil, sharp contact, strong debris simulation, and dangerous near-miss tension. No comedy. No magical powers. No fantasy effects. CAMERA: Use dynamic but readable cinematic camera coverage: - low-angle handheld push-ins - side tracking shots - medium rotating follow shots - brief wide shots for scale and aftermath - one brief slow-motion emphasis only during the most dangerous near-miss. Cuts may be dynamic, but action must remain clear, coherent, and easy to follow. TIMELINE: 0.0–3.8s — Opening 3-hit pressure sequence Start immediately in motion with no pause. The clown launches an aggressive three-part attack sequence: 1. a huge horizontal axe sweep toward Pyona, 2. an immediate reverse backhand sweep, 3. a heavy downward slam. The first swing scrapes the ground and throws sparks, broken stone, and debris. Pyona performs a fast lateral slip-step dodge, narrowly avoiding the blade as it passes just beside her shoulder. Without pause, the clown reverses into the second sweep. Pyona ducks and twists her upper body under the returning blade, avoiding it by centimeters. The clown then commits into the third attack, a brutal overhead slam. Pyona instantly side-jumps or rolls diagonally away just before impact. The axe slams into the ground and carves a trench, blasting dust and rubble upward. The whole opening must feel relentless, like Pyona is under constant lethal pressure with no breathing room. 3.8–6.8s — Fast first counter / control disruption As the axe is briefly stuck or slowed from the heavy slam, Pyona instantly takes the opening. She leaps lightly off broken rubble or a damaged concrete edge and darts in with a short, sharp three-hit counter: - first, a quick strike to the clown’s weapon-control arm or axe-handle area, - second, another fast disrupting hit to the grip / forearm zone, - third, a clean straight punch to the clown’s chest. These hits are not about overpowering the clown, but about disrupting his balance and force generation. The clown stumbles backward and crashes against part of a ruined wall, creating visible cracks and shaking loose concrete dust. Pyona lands and immediately side-steps out again, never staying directly in front of the axe. 6.8–10.2s — Most dangerous near-miss The clown recovers faster than expected and instantly retaliates with a violent reverse horizontal axe sweep aimed directly at Pyona’s torso. This is the most dangerous beat of Scene 1. Use one brief slow-motion emphasis here. Pyona sharply twists her waist and slides half a step sideways at the very last moment. The axe blade misses her body by only a few centimeters, slicing past the side of her outfit and strongly disturbing the hem / edge of her cropped top and jacket without actually injuring her. Ground fragments and dust fly through frame. The near miss must feel terrifyingly close. The slow motion should be very brief, then snap back to full speed immediately. 10.2–12.8s — Clean momentum-based knockout counter Using the dodge momentum from the near miss, Pyona spins fluidly into a fast, powerful roundhouse kick. The kick lands cleanly and forcefully on the clown’s side face / jaw. The impact must feel sharp and real. The clown is blasted backward and crashes through part of a ruined wall or major concrete section. Broken concrete, rubble chunks, and a heavy dust burst explode across the frame. Pyona lands balanced and alert, not posing, already ready for the next move. 12.8–15.0s — Cliffhanger ending Wide aftermath shot. Dust and debris settle around the collapsed wall area. For a brief moment it seems Pyona has finally created space. Then movement appears inside the wreckage. The clown plants one hand into the rubble, grabs the giant axe, and forcefully rises again from the debris, still dangerous and not defeated. Pyona remains in the foreground or mid-ground in a guarded stance, breathing hard, watching him carefully and maintaining distance. End on the clown partially risen in the rubble with the axe in hand, clearly setting up Scene 2. AUDIO: No dialogue. Use only strong cinematic fight sound design: heavy axe swings, metal scrape on stone, ground carving, sparks, concrete cracking, rubble spray, debris fall, air displacement, fast footwork, body impact, wall collapse, dusty low-end impact booms, and tense dark action ambience. No spoken lines. IMPORTANT RULES: Pyona must never tank the axe blade. She must always evade first, then counter. The clown’s axe must feel extremely heavy and lethal. The clown’s attacks must come in chained pressure, not one isolated swing at a time. Pyona’s counters must be short, precise, and opening-based. Environmental damage must match the axe impacts. Keep both characters visually consistent with image1 and image2. Scene 1 must end with the clown rising again, not fully defeated. NEGATIVE: No direct body-blocking of the axe, no weak impact, no fake contact, no floating, no teleporting, no wire-fu, no random extra enemies, no comedy, no magical powers, no repeated identical attack loops, no unreadable blurry action, no identity drift, no hairstyle change, no outfit redesign, no loss of the visible crop-top feel in Pyona’s outfit, no soft or low-energy choreography.
```

> A comedic cinematic video prompt for Seedance 2.0 featuring a high-stakes chase for a hotpot restaurant queue number involving a flying sword and a bicycle.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
[Generation Mode]

Seedance 2.0 Fast

[Video Specifications]

Strictly generate a 15-second video, 16:9 aspect ratio, with three clean shots.

[Overall Style]

Cinematic realistic texture, merging silent film chase comedy rhythm, modern heist film spatial coordination, elegant Xianxia action language, and restrained deadpan performance. Employs a clear audience-misdirection structure: the opening makes viewers believe the two characters are pursuing a priceless mysterious object, only to reveal at the end they are just trying to keep their hotpot restaurant queue spot.

[Characters]

Character ID A | Sword Immortal Lady

Sword Immortal Lady @Image 1, the same East Asian female aged 25-30, oval face, natural fair skin, sharp dark brown eyes, long straight black hair, tall and slender build. Wearing white cloth boots, flowing white embroidered silk Hanfu, semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, silver waist ornament, and a jade hairpin. Strictly maintain the same identity from the reference image.

Character ID B | Bicycle Lady

Bicycle Lady @Image 2, the same East Asian female aged 25-30, rounded and expressive face, brown short ponytail, yellow jacket, blue jeans, white sneakers, reference accessories, and the same bicycle. Strictly maintain the same identity from the reference image.

[Core Props]

The same paper queue number, the same silver long sword, and the same bicycle.

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Low-angle Wide Tracking Shot]

A bustling riverside commercial plaza at blue hour. Warm red lanterns hang outside a crowded hotpot restaurant. Wet stone pavement forms clear reflections. Outdoor waiting stools, steam-covered glass windows, parked e-bikes, wind-blown trees, scattered tissues, and strong night winds constitute a stable environment.

A small paper queue number slips from Character B's hand and is blown toward the plaza's depths.

The two stare at it in panic, and Character A says in a low voice: "Chase."

[Shot 2 | 5-10s | Medium Side Tracking Chase Shot]

The same woman in white silk Hanfu stands on the same silver long sword, gliding stably at a height of half a meter from the ground; the same woman in a yellow jacket on a bicycle rides rapidly beside her.

The same paper number rolls and flies through the red lantern plaza; Character A uses a wide sleeve to change local wind direction while Character B rings the bicycle bell to make pedestrians yield. They maintain precise coordination without collision.

Heroic drum beats, sword hums, bicycle chain rhythms, and strong wind sounds together form an exaggerated action score.

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Close-up into Wide Reversal Shot]

The same Sword Immortal Lady leaps from the silver sword and accurately pinches the paper number just one centimeter before it hits a puddle.

The heroic music suddenly stops.

The hotpot restaurant's loudspeaker announces: "Number A108, please enter immediately; expired numbers must re-queue."

The two look at the number, then at the restaurant, and shout in unison: "Charge!"

They immediately return at top speed, with the silver sword and bicycle advancing side-by-side.

The frame freezes on their dead-serious faces as Character B shouts: "Immortal cultivation can wait, but hotpot cannot!"

[Technical Requirements]

Strict total duration of 15 seconds, three clean shots, 16:9 aspect ratio, stable character identities, continuous prop trajectories. Realistic physics for strong wind, paper, bicycles, hair, and silk fabrics. Original synchronized Mandarin dialogue, loudspeaker announcements, environmental sounds, and music. No on-screen subtitles.

[Negative Prompts]

blurry, bad quality, low quality, low resolution, noisy, jpeg artifacts, watermark, text, error; deformed, mutated, bad anatomy, poorly drawn hands, bad composition, out of frame, disfigured; inconsistent character, changing clothes, face morphing, background shift, glitching cuts, disappearing props

### Summer Beach BBQ and Anime Character Sticker

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

> A complex multi-part video prompt for Seedance that blends live-action beach BBQ footage with a 2D anime sticker character, including detailed instructions for character consistency and interaction.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
[Seedance 2.5 / 15 seconds / 9:16]

Use the reference image as the sole character reference. 15 seconds, 9:16 vertical, one cut, fixed camera. The camera is completely fixed from start to finish. Zooming, panning, tilting, rotating, handheld movement, cuts, transitions, reframing, slow motion, and freeze-frame effects are prohibited.

[Composition/Positioning]
Place a live-action BBQ grill and meat on the bone in the left 45% of the screen. Place a 2D character in the right 30% of the screen. Reserve about 20% of space in the center for tongs and moving the meat.

The character stands on the sandy beach on the right, always displaying their whole body from head to black flip-flops. Do not place the character in front of the grill, on top of the grill legs, or on the table. Leave clear space between the character and the grill, maintaining their standing position from start to finish.

Do not overlap the grill, meat, human hands, tongs, or gochujang bottle with the character's body. The character must not hide the meat or grill. Human hands or tongs must not unnaturally cross the character's face or body. Accurately maintain depth and foreground-background relationships between objects.

[Visual Style]
A comical video combining live-action BBQ footage at a beautiful midsummer seaside with a flat 2D animated sticker character.

Draw a blue sea, white waves, bright blue sky, golden sand, a distant coastline, and palm leaves swaying in the wind. Bright daytime natural light. Palm leaves, grill smoke, and the hem of the character's white shirt sway naturally in the sea breeze.

The background, sea, sand, grill, charcoal fire, smoke, flame, meat on the bone, fat, meat juice, gochujang, bottle, human hand, and metal tongs are all high-quality live-action expressions. Blown-out highlights, excessive bloom, haze, and exposure flickering are prohibited.

Only the character remains a flat 2D animated sticker until the end. Live-action, semi-live-action, 3D, CG figure, game model, Pixar style, or clay animation styles are prohibited. Maintain clear line drawings, cell shading, flat shadows, and thin white borders.

[Character Persistence]
Use the male character from the reference image exactly as they are. Fix the soft ash-silver wavy hair, bangs shape, hair length and volume, grey-blue eyes, young well-formed face, and slim long-limbed body type.

Fix the white sleeveless innerwear, sheer white short-sleeved open shirt, white drawcord, black paisley shorts, black flip-flops, silver necklace, earrings, bracelet, and ring.

Do not change the face, hairstyle, body type, clothing, patterns, colors, shoes, or accessories. Do not add hats, sunglasses, aprons, gloves, or new props. Do not duplicate the character. Do not add another 2D character.

The character is not coldly expressionless but moves very cutely and expressively. Clearly show large shining eyes, rosy cheeks, a happy smile, tilting the head, small shoulder-shaking movements, a sulking face with puffed cheeks, teary eyes, and frantic waving of hands. No standing stiffly.

[BBQ and Meat on the Bone]
Grill a large Tomahawk steak on the bone in the live-action BBQ grill on the left side of the screen. Long bones are clearly visible and physically connected to the meat. The meat is well-grilled, showing deep sear marks, savory char, glossy juices, melted fat, and light steam.

Do not make the meat raw, charred black, boneless, or deformed. Do not duplicate meat or bones. Do not let bones disappear midway.

A live-action adult woman's hand enters from the left edge of the screen. Maintain pink nail art, thin silver rings, and normal five fingers. Turn and lift the meat with metal BBQ tongs. Do not fuse the hand, fingers, ring, tongs, meat, or grill.

[Gochujang]
Use a transparent squeeze bottle with no label, text, or brand logo. The content is thick, glossy, deep reddish-brown gochujang. Do not make it bright red like ketchup or a watery liquid.

The gochujang flows in a thick continuous stream from the bottle nozzle and falls directly onto the meat surface. Do not make the sauce appear suddenly from mid-air. Do not leak from the bottom or sides of the bottle.

[00:00–00:03]
Start with a fixed wide composition. On the left, a Tomahawk steak on the bone is sizzling loudly. A woman's live-action hand slowly turns the meat once with metal tongs. Fat falls onto the charcoal, and a small flame rises for a moment. Thin smoke drifts in the sea breeze.

The 2D character on the right stares at the meat, their eyes sparkling large. They put their hands together under their chin, raise their shoulders, bend their knees slightly, and bounce twice. A small 2D drop of drool appears at the mouth for a moment and disappears.

[00:03–00:05]
The character finds the gochujang bottle. Their eyes sparkle, they tilt their head, and point at the bottle with one hand. They happily hold the bottle with both hands and hug it to their chest for a moment.

Then, with a mischievous smile and one eye closed, they point the nozzle toward the meat on the left. The character themselves does not move from their standing position on the right.

[00:05–00:07]
The character squeezes the bottle hard, pouring a large amount of gochujang onto the meat on the bone. A thick reddish-brown sauce extends continuously from the nozzle to the meat, creating a large zigzag pattern on the meat surface.

The sauce bubbles lightly with the heat and mixes with the meat juice to create a gloss. Maintain visibility of the bone and grill marks without completely hiding the whole meat.

The character smiles proudly, sticks out their tongue slightly, and shakes their shoulders cutely while squeezing the bottle. The woman's hand stops in surprise while holding the tongs.

[00:07–00:09]
The woman's hand quickly takes the gochujang bottle with the tongs and returns it to the left. Immediately after, she lightly taps the top of the character's head once with the tip of the closed tongs.

The tip of the tongs clearly and lightly touches the character's hair. At the moment of contact, a small yellow 2D star-shaped impact effect appears over the head. Do not hit hard. Do not make it painful. Make it a completely cute cartoon-style reprimand.

The moment they are hit, the character's head tilts to the right, their silver hair bounces, and their shoulders and torso shake greatly to the left and right. Knees bend, and they spread their arms to balance. Feet do not leave the sand, and they do not move toward the grill.

They puff their cheeks, close their eyes tight, and shed a single small tear. Clearly show the cute shaking movement of the whole body, not just the head, for about 1 second.

[00:09–00:11]
The woman's hand returns the tongs to the grill and lifts a bite-sized piece of grilled meat with gochujang on it. The lifted meat shows grill marks, juice, fibers, and thick gochujang.

The tongs pass through the center space and carry the meat to the front of the character's mouth. The character sulks about being hit on the head, crosses their arms, turns their face away, and puffs their cheeks.

Immediately they glance at the meat with just their eyes. Their eyes sparkle again, they uncross their arms, put both hands behind their back, and lean their upper body slightly forward. They open their mouth wide and approach the meat.

[00:11–00:12]
The character clearly [eats] the meat
```

```
Red and White Swordswoman Red Energy Sword Combat Combo
Scene Header: Day · Interior · White Geometric Space 
 · monochrome minimalist · high-key diffuse light · red energy accent

Shot 1 | 0-2s · Medium Shot · Stance
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman stands in the center of a white geometric space. Long white hair, Cheongsam-style outfit, red flower ornament. A red energy blade materializes in the right hand, in a floating ready state. The left hand is lowered naturally in a relaxed posture.
Dialogue: None

Shot 2 | 2-4s · Medium Shot · Spin Slash
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman twists her body clockwise on the axis of her waist. The red energy blade in the right hand draws a large arc from the right side forward for a slash. A red translucent motion blur trail leaves a trajectory of the sword. Her white long hair and Cheongsam flow due to the centrifugal force of the rotation.
Dialogue: None

Shot 3 | 4-6s · Low Angle Medium Shot · Low Sweep
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman sinks into a low posture with her knees on the ground. The red energy blade rotates horizontally around her body just above the ground, drawing a wide semicircular arc. Red light reflects on the floor of the white geometric space.
Dialogue: None

Shot 4 | 6-8s · Medium Shot · Upward Slash to Mid-air Jump
Visual: As the Red and White Swordswoman rises from the low posture, the red energy blade draws a vertical arc from bottom to top. She jumps into the air, and her body rises. The energy blade follows and floats above her head.
Dialogue: None

Shot 5 | 8-10s · Medium Shot · Mid-air Inverted Slash
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman flips her body in the air. The red energy blade draws a trajectory smashing downward from above her head. Her body rotates elegantly in the air, and her long white hair flows. The pillars of the white geometric space flow in the background.
Dialogue: None

Shot 6 | 10-12s · Low Angle Medium Shot · Landing Impact
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman lands in a low posture with one hand on the ground. The red energy blade strikes the ground, creating a shockwave with orange sparks and red light particles scattering. The afterglow of the landing impact remains in the white geometric space.
Dialogue: None

Shot 7 | 12-14s · Medium Shot · Return to Stance
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman stands up, the red energy blade returns to her right hand, and the energy converges. She returns to an upright stance. The silence of the white geometric space returns.
Dialogue: None

SFX: Energy resonance sound + sword wind sound + landing impact sound + sparking fire sounds

Visual style direction: monochrome minimalist environment with red energy accent, high-key diffuse lighting, cel-shaded 3D anime aesthetic, translucent red arc motion trails for energy blade trajectory, orange spark particles and red light particles on impact, elegant and precise combat choreography.
```

### Authentic Korean Samgyupsal Vlog

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A highly detailed multi-shot video prompt for a realistic food vlog, utilizing character reference images and specific timestamp-based actions.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
7-shot handheld phone vlog with snapshot realism, slight tilt, natural handheld shake, warm indoor lighting, fine film grain, photorealistic. A woman Image1 eats lunch alone at a rustic Korean samgyupsal restaurant. Image1 defines only her face and hairstyle. She wears a loose olive-green cotton short-sleeve shirt tucked into dark trousers. She remains seated on the same side facing the camera throughout. Only her face is visible; the grill attendant appears only as hands. A wooden table holds a charcoal grill, tongs, scissors, ssamjang, garlic, sesame oil, lettuce, and napkins. Thick pork belly sizzles as smoke rises under warm pendant lights. Natural, subtle reactions throughout. Shot 1 (0–2s): Hands place pork belly on the grill. She watches the sizzle and says, "오 소리 봐." Camera gently pulls back. Shot 2 (2–4s): Overhead shot as she cuts the pork with scissors on the grill. Slight handheld shake. Seedance 2.0 Fast

Character ID B | Junior Sister

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Wide Shot or Long Shot]

The background remains the same mountain wall, pine tree, floating mist, stone platform, and distant roofed water pavilion; the incorrect vertical orientation must be maintained without altering character anatomy.

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Close-up or Extreme Close-up]

Extreme Close-up: The Senior Sister's hard-maintained dignified smile instantly freezes, one eyebrow twitches slightly, and she then replies as if nothing happened: "This is to test your eyesight."

Ends with a dry pluck of a guqin string and the sound of a pine cone falling onto the stone slab.

FORMAT 15s / free rhythm / 1 MATCH CUT / CONTINUOUS MOVE UNTIL MATCH CUT + IMMEDIATE ACTION FROM FIRST FRAME

SUBJECTS: A lone astronaut in a damaged EVA suit desperately repairs a failing spacecraft while floating above Earth. The same person is later revealed sitting in a small apartment wearing casual clothes, where a VR headset appears only after the match cut and is removed in one uninterrupted motion.

ENVIRONMENT: Outer space above Earth’s glowing blue atmosphere, drifting debris, blinking warning lights, frozen silence broken by suit alarms. Transition into a dim but cozy apartment filled with scattered electronics, coffee mugs, and soft morning light spilling through curtains.

MOOD: High-stakes survival dissolves into everyday reality without breaking movement or spatial continuity.

COLOR LOGIC: Naturalistic Film Print Emulation.

⸻

TIMELINE

0:00–0:07

One continuous handheld-style zero-gravity camera move, WS gradually collapsing into MCU. The astronaut tumbles toward a damaged satellite panel as warning lights flash across the visor. One gloved hand desperately reaches for a sparking cable while the other stabilizes against the hull. Loose debris slowly spins past the lens. The Earth fills half the background, slowly rotating beneath. The camera floats with the subject, maintaining believable inertia and subtle drift while slowly shifting from a 24mm to 35mm perspective.

Without cutting, the camera tightens into a dead-on CU. Sparks burst inches from the astronaut’s helmet as a damaged panel begins separating from the spacecraft. Just before impact, a woman’s voice calmly calls,

“Alex…”

Then louder,

“ALEX!”

The astronaut exhales in annoyance instead of panic.

Everything suddenly enters slow motion.

Floating debris nearly freezes.

The separating panel hangs motionless.

Only the astronaut continues moving at normal speed.

The camera slowly orbits clockwise around the helmet while maintaining identical framing and distance.

Instead of reaching for the failing equipment, both hands rise toward the helmet in one smooth, deliberate motion.

No headset or gaming device is visible anywhere in space.

Maintain identical face size, hand position, lens distance, head angle, and clockwise motion until the match cut.

SFX: (radio distortion stretching into silence, slowed warning alarms, breathing, woman’s voice echoing “Alex… ALEX…”, ambient hum fading)

⸻

0:11–0:15

MATCH CUT.

CU to MS.

The rising hands occupy the exact same screen position as the helmet instantly becomes a VR headset in a modest apartment.

The motion never stops.

For the first time, the VR headset is visible.

The subject grips both sides and smoothly lifts

TABLE RIBBON SPREAD AND PROGRESSIVE TURNOVER Strictly reference the uploaded "Ribbon Spread" video for the movement only. Use an overhead 90-degree top-down view. The frame contains only both hands and the full ribbon spread on the green felt table. The original forms chase these shadows, but the shadows mutate faster—becoming brighter, thicker, and more dominant until they surpass the objects themselves. [6–7.5s] A rapid chain reaction begins. Colliding forms generate visual motifs derived from the reference imagery: fragments, symbols, silhouettes, stains, and expressive linework. These newly born motifs accelerate in different directions. Frozen traces solidify into new entities before melting again and splitting into tiny emotional echoes. --- [7.5–15s: Compression and Extreme Transformation] [7.5–9s] The entire frame collapses toward an invisible point beneath the center. Every visual layer compresses inward at a different speed. Maximum compression lasts for only one frame before exploding upward and outward with vertical smears, shaking outlines, and exaggerated overshoot motion. Every element vibrates before settling into a completely new position. [9–10.5s] Textures migrate between forms. Dry rough textures sharpen previously fluid shapes into angular structures. Liquid textures soften rigid forms until they sag and drip. Luminous colorful textures dissolve into the background, causing the environment itself to pulse rhythmically. Every form flips, inverts, or turns inside out, exposing hidden reverse sides possessing qualities of hands, hooks, flames, or waves. Every collision triggers escalating chain reactions, transforming the entire visual world into an entirely new emotional reality. [12–13.5s] All boundaries dissolve. Forms merge, repel, and merge again, generating hybrid entities that inherit the colors and movement of their predecessors while becoming completely different beings. The background pushes forward into the foreground, becoming part of the forms themselves while releasing new shapes from within. [13.5–15s] Every previous process returns simultaneously at maximum intensity: contour separation, color bleeding, texture migration, and shadow-leading motion. Every frame is densely packed with intentional visual impact, fully absorbing and reinterpreting every reference element introduced earlier. --- [15–22.5s: Fragmentation and Neurotic Storm] [15–16.5s] The storm collides with an invisible barrier. The center explodes into countless shards of water-covered broken glass. Within each fragment, glowing spiral irises rotate violently, generating centrifugal force that stretches and tears at the shard edges. Neon magenta and electric blue leak through every crack. [16.5–18s] The fragments fuse into overlapping collaged human faces. The faces are composed of purple stains, scratched paint, and rough pigment. Their expressions oscillate continuously between terror and ecstasy. Jagged lightning-like lines pierce through them like exposed nerve fibers, violently stitching the faces together. [18–19.5s] Color saturation reaches its peak. Electric blue lines solidify into physical cables stretching throughout space, twitching uncontrollably while releasing neon magenta sparks. The compressed paper background burns away, transforming into a charred, granular carbon-black surface. [19.5–21s] Gravity reverses. Liquid elements such as water droplets and wet ink surge upward while broken glass continues falling downward, producing intense visual tearing. [21–22.5s] Every form is pulled into countless miniature spiral-iris black holes. The irises rapidly expand until they dominate the entire screen, spinning at an increasingly suffocating speed. The image breathes with a neurotic rhythm. Hidden symbols and textures appear and disappear beneath muscular contractions. Paint seepage creates convincing depth, as though layered across multiple transparent sheets of film. [24–25.5s] The animation enters an intense high-frequency vibration. Every object freezes in place while its edges oscillate violently like strings stretched to their breaking point. Violet shadows crawl across every surface, attempting to consume the remaining light. [25.5–27s] Rainbow-colored glass fragments and water droplets sweep across the frame like rain, refracting and distorting everything behind them through lens-like optical effects. Colors become heavy fluid masses that sink downward, leaving behind anxious, restless linear marks. [27–28.5s] The entire composition collapses into a single glowing horizontal jagged line. The background becomes a dark neon field filled with coarse grain. The line contains all accumulated energy, twisting violently while alternating flashes of electric blue and neon magenta pulse across it. [28.5–30s] The glowing line disintegrates into hand-drawn dust particles that drift rhythmically across the screen before gradually reassembling into one enormous blurred luminous silhouette. Strictly replicate the movement logic of the reference video 'Balance Outfit Change': the female lead balances on one leg, uses the outer side of her lifted foot to hold a bundle of 'shoes + clothes', tosses the bundle into the air, quickly retracts her leg to build power, and performs a single high-speed kick that accurately hits the target; the outfit change occurs in the exact frame where the foot and the clothes overlap. The change must be a hard cut in one frame, not a gradient, transformation, or clothes gradually covering the body. No dialogue, no narration, no subtitles, no watermarks, keep the background music.
[Character Lock] The lead is an adult woman over 25, using @1. Facial features, skin tone, hairstyle, height, and body proportions remain consistent throughout, no face-swapping, no age change, no body shape change. Movements feature realistic center of gravity, muscle exertion, breathing patterns, and slight balance swaying.
[Outfit Lock] Before change: Black short-sleeved T-shirt, lavender flare pants, black and white sneakers. After change: The lead instantly switches to the complete look of preset #1, strictly maintaining the colors, cut, material, and styling of #1. Only one outfit change occurs in the entire video, and the second look lasts until the end without adding, removing, or replacing any clothing, shoes, or accessories.
[Starting Clothing Bundle] The opening prop is not a single shoe, but a compact, weighted bundle consisting of the shoes and folded clothes corresponding to preset #1. The shoe outlines are on top, with folded fabric supporting the shoes from below and some fabric edges hanging naturally. The bundle is supported by the outer side of the lead's left sneaker, the outer instep, and near the ankle. It is not placed on the toe, not worn, and not floating. Once tossed, the shoes and clothes must fly together as a single unit without scattering.
[Scene] Minimalist modern residential interior with white walls and light gray hard flooring. Background includes a white wardrobe, door, washing machine, and a few household items. Natural window light mixed with soft indoor light; furniture, background, and lighting direction remain constant. Realistic mobile phone video texture, skin retains natural texture, clothing and shoe materials are clear.
[Close-up Fixed Camera] Fixed tight full-body shot, camera distance approximately 2.1–2.3 meters from the lead, height about 1.05 meters, 30–32mm equivalent focal length, nearly eye-level with a slight low angle. The lead occupies about 80% of the screen height, positioned slightly to the right to leave space for the left leg lift and clothing bundle trajectory. About 8% space at the top and 4% at the bottom. The person, support leg, lifted left leg, and airborne bundle stay fully in frame. Stationary throughout, no zooming, panning, or tracking cuts.
[Strict Time Storyboard] [00:00-00:00.90] The lead stands sideways, right leg as the sole support, right knee slightly bent; left leg lifted sideways to near horizontal. Left foot turned slightly inward so the outer side of the sneaker faces up like a tilted tray. The bundle of shoes and clothes is held steadily by the outer foot. She looks at the bundle with focus. Arms naturally raised for balance. No eye contact with camera.
[00:00.90-00:01.05] A short, clear upward flick using only the left ankle and outer foot, tossing the bundle diagonally upward. A light toss, not a full kick. The bundle leaves the foot, rising along a short parabolic path. Fabric edges expand slightly due to air, but the unit stays together. Accompanying 'whoosh' sound.
[00:01.05-00:01.45] Bundle rises to peak. Lead immediately retracts left leg, knee bending toward chest/abdomen in a compact ready stance. Body tilts slightly right, arms spread with gravity shift. Eyes track the clothes, no looking at camera.
[00:01.45-00:01.65] Bundle begins to descend. Lead finishes retracting leg, pausing for no more than 0.2s to judge the landing point.
[00:01.65-00:01.85] Lead performs one formal high kick, succeeding on the first try. Left leg explodes diagonally upward, knee opening rapidly, shin and toe fully extending in 0.2s. Fast, clean, sharp kick with natural motion blur. Left foot accurately meets the falling bundle, no misses or corrections.
[00:01.85, Instant Hard Cut] Frame of contact between foot and bundle center triggers a seamless hard cut. Pre-contact frame: Lead in black T-shirt and lavender pants; bundle in air. Post-contact frame: Lead in preset #1; bundle completely gone. No transition frames, no blending, no gradual growth. Just a hard cut.
[00:01.85-00:02.80] Hold high kick peak for 0.1s, then lower leg. She looks at her foot first, then slowly looks at the camera with confident smile.
[00:02.80-00:04.10] First siren-style fashion pose: S-curve body, one hand on waist, the other passing through hair. Looks away then back at camera with a mysterious smile.
[00:04.10-00:05.40] Two-thirds profile stance, feet staggered, shoulders back. She looks at camera, tilts head, and blinks slowly with a playful, high-fashion vibe.
[00:05.40-00:06.60] Slow turn showing back to camera. Hair slides along shoulders. Turns head back over shoulder with a slight smirk.
[00:06.60-00:09.30] Walks away from camera toward depth of room with 3-4 steps of a relaxed runway gait. Fixed camera captures full back view.
[00:09.30-00:10.40] Stops in background, one foot pointed sideways, still back to camera, then looks over shoulder with a mysterious side-smile and quick wink.
[00:10.40-00:12.00] Climax: On the final beat, a clean half-turn with a hair flip, stepping toward camera into a two-thirds frontal pose. One hand on waist, other near chin, chin up, staring at camera with direct siren gaze. Final 0.3s freeze frame.
[Audio] 00:00-01:85 ethereal female humming with reverb, ambient friction/whoosh sounds. At 01:85, heavy electronic drum drop with a deep 'thump'. 01:85-12:00 continuous electronic beat with walking/clothing friction sounds. Ending with an upward sweep. No dialogue.
[Strict Negative Constraints] No gradient changes, no dissolving, no gradual clothes growth, no fabric crawling on body, no color spreading, no old clothes fading; no intermediate states. Hard cut at contact: 100% old outfit before, 100% preset #1 after. No unauthorized modification to #1.

Seedance 2.0 Fast

Movie-level realistic texture, using classic adventure film-style visual misdirection, silent film-style deadpan rhythm, stormy coastal atmosphere, restrained character performance, clear foreground and background spatial scheduling, and scale revelation driven by camera movement; comedy comes from two characters handling a harmless object with an overly serious heroic posture, with a second visual reversal added at the end.

Character ID A | Sword Immortal Female

Use @Image 1 as strict identity reference for Character ID A: the same 25–30 year old East Asian Sword Immortal female, oval face, natural fair skin, sharp dark brown eyes, black long straight hair, tall and slender figure, wearing white cloth boots, flowing white embroidered silk Hanfu, semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, silver waist ornaments, wearing a jade hairpin and carrying a silver long sword.

Character ID B | Cycling Female

Use @Image 2 as strict identity reference for Character ID B: the same 25–30 year old East Asian cycling female, rounded and expressive face, brown short ponytail, yellow windbreaker, blue jeans, white sneakers, black helmet, and the same bicycle.

[Core Props]

The same silver long sword, the same bicycle, a red long rope, a giant inflatable dragon kite, and a larger pink pig kite.

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Low Angle Wide Shot]

A seaside rooftop in the evening wind, with wet concrete ground, metal railings, wind-torn warning tape, distant tall buildings, black clouds, sea fog, and fierce gusts establishing the environment.

A huge black flying dragon silhouette struggles wildly above the city skyline, lightning illuminating its spread wings, accompanied by low roars.

Character A enters the frame fully from head to toe, drawing the silver long sword; Character B steadies her bicycle beside her, both looking up with solemn expressions.

The camera slowly and steadily pushes in; accompanied by thunder, fabric flapping, chain clicking, and low orchestral drum beats.

[Shot 2 | 5-10s | Medium Side Tracking Shot]

The same Sword Immortal female in white embroidered silk Hanfu throws the same silver long sword into the air, while the same cycling female in the yellow windbreaker pedals the same bicycle quickly, tightening a red rope connected to the "dragon."

The same rooftop, unified wind direction, metal railings, red rope, and city skyline remain consistent.

The silver long sword accurately hooks into the rope loop on the monster's wing; both pull back simultaneously, white cloth boots and bicycle tires realistically sliding on the wet ground as the giant dragon gradually descends.

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Reveal Close-up to Extreme Close-up]

The same two women carefully lower the kite to the ground, simultaneously sighing in relief.

A child's voice comes from off-camera: "Auntie, mine is the pink pig!"

The two turn their heads slowly.

Focus shifts behind them, where an even larger pink pig kite is drifting further away with the wind.

Extreme close-up of their synchronized blank expressions; the silver long sword silently returns to its scabbard automatically.

[Technical Requirements]

No dissolves, morphing transitions or continuous camera orbit. MASTER DRAGON LOCK: Show one enormous ancient dragon with a broad powerful body, charcoal-black overlapping scales, subtle dark crimson colouring across its head and neck, two swept-back horns, smaller facial spikes, glowing amber-orange eyes, one long tail and one pair of enormous bat-like wings with dark leathery membranes. The dragon has four legs: two forelegs and two hind legs. Its design, size, horns, colour, anatomy and facial structure remain identical in all three shots. The dragon must feel extremely heavy, ancient and physically realistic. MASTER RIDER LOCK: One adult armoured rider is already seated securely in a dark leather saddle on the dragon from the very first frame. The rider wears detailed weathered silver plate armour, dark under-clothing, a fitted medieval helmet with a narrow shadowed visor and a long dark cloak. The rider’s face is never clearly visible. The rider holds leather reins connected to the saddle and remains seated throughout the entire sequence. The rider does not suddenly appear, mount, dismount or change clothing. During the first shot, the rider is naturally hidden behind the dragon’s large head, neck and folded wings. ENVIRONMENT AND LIGHTING: The sequence takes place inside a gigantic ancient mountain cave. The cave contains rough black rock walls, enormous stone formations, drifting mist, scattered bones and faint glowing embers between cracks in the ground. The far interior is almost black, but the dragon must remain readable through soft amber reflections and cold blue rim light entering from the cave mouth. The cave entrance opens from a high mountain cliff into a vast dramatic landscape of jagged mountains, deep valleys, distant waterfalls, rolling clouds and pale stormy daylight. Maintain a cinematic cold-blue and muted-amber colour palette with deep shadows, volumetric light rays and realistic atmospheric haze. Dark and mysterious, but never so underexposed that the dragon or rider becomes blurry or invisible. SHOT 1 — DRAGON EMERGES, 0–5 SECONDS: Begin with a symmetrical low frontal wide shot positioned near the cave entrance, facing deep into the darkness. The camera slowly tracks backward toward the entrance as the enormous dragon walks forward out of the black cave interior. The dragon’s wings remain completely folded against both sides of its body. Its glowing amber eyes appear first, followed by its horned head, shoulders and powerful forelegs becoming visible through the mist. Each heavy footstep shakes the cave floor and dislodges small stones and dust from the ceiling. The dragon exhales dense vapour from its nostrils and releases a deep restrained growl. Its head moves naturally with the weight of each step. The dragon looks directly past the camera toward the cave opening. The rider is already seated but remains hidden behind the dragon’s head and neck. Do not reveal the rider clearly during this shot. SHOT 2 — RIDER REVEAL, 5–9 SECONDS: Clean hard cut to a low three-quarter side tracking shot beside the moving dragon. Begin close to the dragon’s horned head and sharply detailed scales. Track smoothly backward along the dragon’s neck and folded wing while the dragon continues walking toward the cave entrance. As the camera reaches the dragon’s shoulder, reveal the single armoured rider seated in the saddle between the folded wings. Hold the composition long enough to clearly show the rider’s silver armour, helmet, dark cloak and both hands gripping the reins. The rider looks toward the bright cave exit and leans slightly forward in preparation for takeoff. The dragon and rider remain in continuous forward motion. Do not show a separate rider anywhere else in the cave. SHOT 3 — REAR TAKEOFF AND FLIGHT, 9–15 SECONDS: Clean hard cut to an epic wide rear view positioned behind and slightly above the dragon and rider. The bright cave mouth and vast mountain landscape fill the background ahead of them. The dragon accelerates into a powerful run toward the cave opening while its wings remain folded enough to pass safely through the cave. The dragon reaches the cliff edge and makes one forceful leap completely out of the cave. Only after clearing the rock opening, it spreads both enormous wings to their full symmetrical width. The sudden wing expansion pushes mist, dust and loose stones outward from the cave entrance. The rider leans forward, grips the reins and remains firmly attached to the saddle. The dragon performs one enormous downward wingbeat, catches the air and transitions naturally into stable forward flight. Loose stones fall from the cliff beneath it. The camera rapidly follows from behind as the dragon and rider soar away over the deep valley between jagged mountain peaks. The long tail streams naturally behind the dragon and the rider’s cloak whips in the wind. End on a majestic wide rear silhouette of the dragon flying into the open mountain landscape as sunlight breaks through the storm clouds. CAMERA AND MOTION: Premium cinematic camera movement with realistic scale and parallax. Shot one is a controlled backward dolly. Shot two is a smooth low side-tracking reveal. Shot three is a dynamic rear pursuit shot that follows the takeoff out of the cave. Use natural motion blur only during the leap and wingbeat. Keep the dragon’s movement heavy on the ground and powerful but graceful in the air. No shaky chaotic framing and no extreme close-ups that obscure the action. CINEMATIC QUALITY: Big-budget photorealistic dark-fantasy visual effects, highly detailed scales and armour, realistic creature muscles beneath the skin, physically accurate wing membranes, believable foot contact, convincing dust and falling-rock physics, volumetric cave mist, dramatic rim lighting, strong depth, atmospheric perspective and stable character consistency. AUDIO: No dialogue and no music. Deep dragon breathing, restrained growl, heavy echoing footsteps, claws scraping stone, armour movement, leather saddle creaking, falling pebbles, rushing cave wind, powerful wingbeat and a deep dragon roar as it enters open air. STRICT CONSISTENCY: Exactly one dragon and one rider. The rider is seated on the dragon from the beginning and never disappears. No additional people, dragons or creatures. No dragon transformation or colour change. No extra heads, horns, tails, wings, legs or riders. The wings remain folded inside the cave and open only after the dragon has completely cleared the cave mouth. The wings must not clip through the cave walls. The rider must not float above the saddle or merge into the dragon. No fire breathing, combat, dialogue, text, logos, blood or visible injuries.

### エイリアンのエスプレッソ・コメディ・シークエンス

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> 初めてカフェインを摂取したエイリアンを描く、10 秒間のユーモラスな物語プロンプト。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
エイリアン。エスプレッソ。後悔。

ハイパーリアルなシネマティック・コメディ・シークエンス、10 秒間、16:9。

居心地の良いカフェの中。1 人のエイリアンが、まるで危険な実験サンプルを扱うかのように、慎重に小さなエスプレッソを飲んでいる。0.5 秒間、何も起こらない。次の瞬間、エイリアンの目が大きく見開き、全身が震え出し、パニック状態でこう囁く。「なぜ色が聞こえるんだ？」

エイリアンは突然超高速で動き出し、すべてのテーブルを掃除し、すべての椅子を逆さまに積み上げ、ペストリーをアルファベット順に並べ替え、コーヒーマシンを神のように崇めて敬礼する。エイリアンのエネルギーでエスプレッソマシンが揺れ始め、トイレの中に小さな光るポータルが開く。トイレットペーパーが白い竜巻のように飛び出してくる。

最後は、震えながらテーブルに戻ってきたエイリアンが、空になったエスプレッソカップを指差して「もう一杯」と言って終わる。

スタイル：ハイパーリアル、シネマティック、カオスなコメディ、エイリアン 1 人のみ、カフェ 1 軒のみ、エスプレッソが引き金、超高速パニック、飛び散るトイレットペーパー、トイレのポータル、ホラー要素なし、流血表現なし、テキストなし、ロゴなし、カートゥーン調なし、スローモーションなし、有名な著名人の顔なし。
```

### Mini DV レトロな植物ケア ASMR Vlog

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> 90 年代のビデオカメラ風のノスタルジックな植物ケア動画を生成。手ブレやフォーカス調整の揺らぎを取り入れ、ASMR サウンドと共にリアルな質感を再現します。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
カメラ / ルック：被写体自身が撮影したような、ハンディタイプの Mini DV ビデオカメラ映像。わずかな手ブレ、時折発生するフォーカス調整の揺らぎ、不完全なフレーミング、自然なズーム操作、テープ特有の柔らかい画質、繊細な粒子感、窓際の日差しによるリアルな自動露出の変化。自然な肌のトーン、穏やかなモーションブラー。洗練された映画のような映像ではなく、家庭用ビデオカメラのリアルな質感を重視。
スタイル：心地よい植物ケアのショート Vlog。ASMR 要素を取り入れた穏やかなペースで、会話は最小限に抑え、自然な瞬間を切り取る。土をすくう音、小さな鉢をこする音、多肉植物の葉が触れ合う音、優しく土を叩く音など、日常の心地よいサウンドにフォーカス。
被写体：20 代半ばの女性。シンプルなカーディガンを着用し、髪はルーズなお団子ヘア。アクセサリーは控えめで、ロゴやブランド品は一切なし。短時間の作業を楽しむ、穏やかで明るい雰囲気。
設定：明るい朝の小さなキッチンの窓際。自然光、シンプルなテラコッタの鉢、無地の土袋を使用。フレーム内にブランド名やロゴは一切映さない。
絵コンテ：
→ (3 秒、固定ミドルショット) カメラを窓際に置き、小さな鉢に土をすくう。「この子を植え替えてあげよう。」
→ (3 秒、クローズアップ) 多肉植物を古い鉢から優しく取り出す。
→ (3 秒、ハンディショット) 新しい土に植え、根元を軽く叩く。「よし、これで快適だね。」
→ (3 秒、ディテールショット) 葉についた土を優しく払う。会話なし。
→ (3 秒、温かみのあるラストショット) 鉢を窓際へ戻し、カメラに向かって微笑む。「また次の植物で。」手でレンズを覆い、録画終了。
オーディオノート：土をすくう音や葉が触れ合う音などの環境音をクリアに収録。会話は親しい友人に話しかけるような、控えめでカジュアルなトーン。
リアリズムノート：自然な身のこなし、まばたき、心からの笑顔、不完全なフレーミング、フォーカスブリージング、明るい日差しの変化を再現。著作権のあるキャラクター、ロゴ、ブランド名、著名人は一切含めない。AI 生成や商業的なスタイルではなく、完全にオリジナルの個人的な Vlog コンテンツとして制作。
```

### シネマティックなファッション誌の表紙撮影

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> Seedance 2.0 向けに設計されたプロフェッショナルなビデオプロンプトです。スタジオ照明の切り替え、シンクロしたポージング、そして最終的なエディトリアル・カバーショットまで、ファッション撮影の様子を演出します。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
0:00–0:03 | スタジオ入り

スタジオの照明が次々と点灯する。フラッシュが焚かれる中、彼女は自信に満ちた足取りで白ホリゾントのセットへ歩みを進める。アシスタントが素早くブレザーを整え、フォトグラファーがカメラを構える。素早いウィップパンのトランジションが期待感を高める。

0:03–0:07 | エディトリアル・ポーズ・シークエンス
カメラのフラッシュに合わせて繰り出される、テンポの速いファッションポーズ：
ブレザーのポケットに手を入れる
肩越しに振り返る
ディレクターズチェアに優雅に座る
顎を少し上げ、真っ直ぐに視線を向ける
ランウェイモデルのようにカメラに向かって歩く
各ポーズは 1 秒未満で、大きなシャッター音と白いフラッシュのトランジションに同期する。

0:07–0:11 | カバーモーメント
背景は、シームレスなマッチカットを用いて、ミニマルな白、大胆な黒、メタリックシルバー、深みのあるクリムゾンへと変化する。送風機が彼女の髪をなびかせ、彼女は視線を外すことなく自信を持ってポーズを変えていく。カメラは、超広角のファッションショットと、表情を捉えるビューティー・クローズアップを交互に切り替える。

0:11–0:15 | 最終的なカバーショット
音楽がわずかにスローダウンする。彼女は最後の一撃となる力強いポーズを決め、完全な自信を持ってレンズを真っ直ぐに見つめる。カメラがゆっくりと極端なビューティー・クローズアップへと押し寄り、複数のフラッシュが連続して焚かれる。フレームは高級雑誌の表紙のように静止し、彼女の周囲にエレガントなエディトリアル・タイポグラフィが現れ、最後のビートでブラックアウトする。
```

### 高級フレグランスブティックのコマーシャル

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> ハイエンドな香水店のコマーシャルに向けた、連続トラッキングショットと温かみのある環境光を特徴とする詳細なシネマティックプロンプト。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
スタイル：超フォトリアル、高級コマーシャル、シネマティック、ワンカットの連続トラッキングショット、プレミアムフレグランスブティック、温かみのある環境光、リアルな人間の動き、35mm レンズ、浅い被写界深度、HDR、8K、スムーズなジンバルスタビライゼーション。

シーン 1 (0～3秒) – 入店

フィット感のある黒の T シャツとダークジーンズを着用したスタイリッシュな青年が、高級香水ブティックに入店する。カメラは左前方からのトラッキングアングルで、彼が自信を持って入店する様子を追う。プレミアムなガラス製香水ボトルが並ぶエレガントな木製棚が、温かみのある LED 照明に照らされて輝く。磨き上げられた白い大理石の床が光を反射し、高級感を演出している。

シーン 2 (3～6秒) – 通路を歩く

カメラは彼の横をスムーズに追従し、彼がフレグランスの通路をゆっくりと歩きながら、何気なくコレクションを眺める様子を捉える。何百もの高級香水ボトルが、ライトアップされた棚に整然と並んでいる。ガラスボトルに映り込む柔らかな反射、清潔感のあるモダンなインテリア、シネマティックな被写界深度、リアルな身体の動き、自然な歩行ペース。

シーン 3 (6～10秒) – コレクションの閲覧

カメラがわずかに接近する中、彼は棚の方へ顔を向け、プレミアムなフレグランスを鑑賞する。クローズアップショットが、黄金色の液体が入ったエレガントな香水ボトル、透明度の高いガラス、金属製のキャップを捉える。背景には柔らかなボケ味、温かみのある高級小売店特有の照明、リアルな表情。

シーン 4 (10～13秒) – ヒーローエンディング

彼が自信を持ってディスプレイの前を通り過ぎる間、カメラはゆっくりとズームインする。高級ブティックの奥へと続く空間には、ライトアップされた香水棚とプレミアムなショールームが広がる。スムーズなシネマティックモーション、エレガントな雰囲気、高級ファッションコマーシャルの美学、超リアルなディテール、クリーンな構図、ハイエンドなカラーグレーディング、テキストなし、ウォーターマークなし、ロゴなし。
```

### シネマティックなパリのファッションキャンペーン

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> Seedance 2.0 向けのマルチシーン動画生成プロンプト。パリのファッションキャンペーンを舞台に、スムーズなトランジションと特定のカメラワークを指定しています。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
15 秒間のハイパーリアルなシネマティック・ファッションキャンペーン動画。ヨーロッパ系の女性、明るく輝く肌、シャープな顔立ち、なめらかなポニーテールのウェーブがかったブロンドヘア、小さなダークカラーの長方形サングラス、パールドロップイヤリング、オーバーサイズのライトブルー・黒・赤のグラフィックストリートウェアジャージ、セージグリーンのワイドレッグパンツ、黒のポインテッドトゥヒール。ゴールドのドームスタッズが付いたベビーピンクのなめらかなレザー製ホーボーショルダーバッグを、全シーンを通して腕に自然にかけている。

シーン 1 · 3 秒 全身のワイドショット。パリの石畳の角に一人で立ち、片手でサングラスを調整する。ベビーピンクのバッグを腕に自然にかけ、温かみのあるゴールデンアワーの光。周囲には本物の人々と街の喧騒がぼやけて映り込み、背景にはクラシックなオスマン様式の建物。

シーン 2 · 3 秒 全身ショット。シャンゼリゼ通りの横断歩道を大胆に歩く。片手にアイスコーヒーのカップ、もう一方の腕にはゴールドスタッズ付きのベビーピンクのバッグを揺らし、歌っているかのように口を少し開けている。自信に満ちた足取りで、背後には車と街並みがぼやけて流れる。

シーン 3 · 3 秒 全体的なシネマティック・ワイドショット。パリの壮大な大通りの中央を歩く小さな姿。背景には凱旋門が大きくそびえ立ち、周囲には本物の群衆。腕にかかったベビーピンクのバッグがバーガンディのディテールとして鮮明に見える。

シーン 4 · 3 秒 ミディアムショット。日差しの降り注ぐパリの石畳の通りで立ち止まり、自然発生的な小さなダンスを披露。腕を少し広げ、ベビーピンクのバッグが自然に揺れる。背景にはぼやけたストリートミュージシャン。楽しげで開放的な表情、顔には温かいゴールデンアワーの光。

シーン 5 · 3 秒 ミディアムクローズアップ。パリの地下鉄車両内でポールにつかまり、指一本でサングラスをゆっくりと押し上げる。わずかに笑みを浮かべ、ゴールドのドームスタッズ付きベビーピンクのバッグが腕に鮮明に映る。地下鉄のムードのある温かい頭上照明、背景には他の乗客が自然にぼやけている。

全 5 シーン間はスムーズでシームレスなトランジション。全体的なムード：パリの街の自然で混沌としたエネルギー、ラグジュアリーなストリートウェアの編集、ゴールデンアワーの温かみ。Canon EOS R5 35mm f/1.4 で撮影、Kodak Portra 400 のフィルムトーン、自然なフィルム粒子、手持ちカメラの動き、全シーンに本物の人々が登場、ハイパーリアルな写真の質感。プラスチックのような肌、ロボットのような動き、硬いポーズ、AI 特有の不自然さは一切なし。

避けるべき要素：漫画、CGI、プラスチックのような肌、ロボットのような動き、硬いポーズ、ぼやけた顔、露出オーバー、ウォーターマーク、テキストオーバーレイ、ロゴ、AI 生成特有の見た目、ぎこちない動き、無人の通り、スタジオ背景、偽の群衆、指の数が多い、変形
```

### エイリアンと寝室のシュールなコメディ

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> 寝室でエイリアンと遭遇し、フライパンで応戦するという予期せぬ展開を描いた、短編コメディ用のシネマティックな動画プロンプト。

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
DNA Prompt Core V2 | Seedance 2.0

## 基本設定
ジャンル：シネマティック・コメディ、シュールなユーモア（デッドパン）。
尺：8 秒。
トーン：最初は完全にリアルで静かな雰囲気から始まり、完璧なタイミングで不条理なコメディへと急展開する。IMAX シネマティック品質、超リアル、ハリウッド映画のようなブロッキング。

## シーン
夜のモダンなアパートの寝室。窓から差し込む柔らかな青い月光と、ベッドサイドランプの暖かな光がシネマティックなコントラストを生み出している。白いベッドシーツ、グレーの枕、ミニマルな寝室、リアルな影、穏やかな雰囲気。

## 被写体

### 画像リファレンス 1
24 歳前後の美しい若い女性。表情豊かな卵型の顔、色白の肌、ナチュラルでソフトなメイク、ダークブラウンの長いウェーブヘア、温かみのあるヘーゼル色の瞳、繊細な顔立ち。ゆったりとしたオーバーサイズの黒い T シャツとダークグレーのラウンジショーツを着用。裸足。毛穴まで見えるリアルな肌の質感、寝起きの少し乱れた髪、眠そうな目元。ドキュメンタリーのようなリアリズム、超フォトリアルな 8K 画質。

### 画像リファレンス 2
AAA、本物のグレイ型エイリアン。身長約 135 cm。非常に大きく滑らかな禿げ頭で、リアルなプロポーション。淡いグレーの肌にわずかな緑が混じった色味、細身で引き締まった体、長い腕と指、大きく光沢のある黒いアーモンド型の目、小さな鼻孔、小さな口、耳はない。非常に詳細な肌の毛穴やシワ、超リアルな生物学的テクスチャ。親しみやすい表情。衣服やアクセサリーは一切なし。古典的なエイリアンの目撃談に着想を得たハリウッド品質のデザイン。漫画的、可愛らしい、あるいはデフォルメされた表現は一切禁止。

## 連続した一連のシークエンス

### モーメント 1 | 0.0-1.8 秒
寝室内の足元側からベッドを捉えた IMAX シネマティックなワイドショット。女性がベッドの左側で穏やかに眠っている。室内は完全に静寂。カメラはゆっくりとドリーインする。

### モーメント 2 | 1.8-2.8 秒
女性の顔の超クローズアップにカット。彼女は突然目を見開く。無表情のまま完全に静止し、2 回だけ瞬きをする。ゆっくりとしたカメラのプッシュインが緊張感を高める。

### モーメント 3 | 2.8-4.5 秒
女性の視点からの POV ショット。カメラがゆっくりと右にパンする。AAA（グレイ型エイリアン）が彼女の隣の毛布の下に横たわっており、楽しげな笑顔で長い手を振っている。エイリアンは自然に瞬きをし、まるで旧友に挨拶するかのように無言で手を振り続ける。

### モーメント 4 | 4.5-5.2 秒
ミディアムショットに戻る。完全に真剣な表情を崩さないまま、彼女の右手に重厚な黒い鋳鉄製のフライパンが何もないところから突然出現する。視覚効果なし。説明なし。完璧なデッドパン・コメディのタイミング。

### モーメント 5 | 5.2-6.8 秒
超高速の IMAX ウィップパン。彼女は最大限の力でフライパンを振り抜き、エイリアンの額に直撃させる。金属がぶつかる大きな音。エイリアンは誇張されたスローモーションで部屋の向こう側へと吹き飛ばされ、楽しげな笑顔で手を振り続けたまま、空中で何度も回転する。
```

### Seedance 2.5 Anime Battle PV

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

> A comprehensive 30-second multi-cut prompt for a high-quality cinematic anime battle, featuring consistent character designs and dynamic camera work throughout a 14-shot sequence.

#### 📝 プロンプト

```
30 seconds, 16:9, high-quality Japanese anime-style battle PV. A very flashy and cinematic image, like a trailer for the latest theatrical anime movie. No text on screen. No additional characters. Only 1 protagonist, 1 divine beast, and 1 enemy boss. Depicted as a continuous sequence of events on the same battlefield throughout the 30 seconds. No transition to other spaces. Maintain consistency of the protagonist, divine beast, enemy boss, background, spatial structure, destruction state, and damage expression until the end. 
The camera moves very dynamically throughout. No static cameras. Include clear camera moves in each cut. Actively use orbiting, rapid approach, rapid retreat, rapid ascent, rapid descent, overhead views, low-angle looking up, side-by-side tracking, circling in front of the subject, high-speed dolly shots close to the ground, foreground parallax passing through rubble, crash zooms, Dutch angles, and sharp turns. At the moment of impact, insert a strong impact like a freeze frame for a moment, and immediately move explosively after that.

Protagonist:
Girl Onmyoji 'Akane'. Around 17 years old. Slender and supple build. Pale skin. Golden-amber eyes. Long black pigtails tied with red ribbons. Before summoning, she wears a dark blue school uniform-style outfit. After summoning, she changes into a priest-style combat outfit based on black, white, and deep red. Equipped with a short combat skirt, talisman designs on the cuffs, arm guards, and leg armor. Weapon is a spirit blade formed from red-gold spiritual power. Focused and tense in the first half, cornered in the middle, and showing determination and ferocity in the second half.

Divine Beast:
A large four-legged divine beast. Has a massive lion's mane, muscular limbs like a wolf, two dragon-like horns, and a long tail. Black fur with red-gold glowing lines. Eyes are gold-red. Massive and heavy-set, focusing on charging, claw attacks, roars, and body slams. A presence combining divinity and ferocity. Maintains the same appearance throughout the video.

Enemy Boss:
'Ghost King in Black Armor'. A massive 4-meter class humanoid boss. Heavy black iron armor. Deep red glowing lines like cracks on the armor surface. Two horns curved backward. Burning red eyes. Carries a massive double-edged black greatsword. Manipulates black flames and shockwaves. Movements are heavy but fast, with each hit being very heavy. Possesses the composure and cruelty of a ruler. Maintains the same appearance throughout the video.

Background:
A plaza of night ruins of a shrine. A large summoning magic circle in the center. A collapsed torii gate in the back right. A fallen stone pillar in the front left. Ruins of a broken altar in the back. Cracked stone floor. Scattered rubble. Sky covered with thunderclouds. Maintain this spatial structure during combat. Even if the camera moves greatly, ensure the central summoning circle, the collapsed torii, the fallen pillar, and the altar ruins are shown repeatedly to identify it as the same battlefield.
Any destruction or damage that occurs will be continuously reflected in subsequent cuts. Maintain the broken stone floor, blown-away rubble, the beast's hit marks, cracks in the chest armor of the Ghost King, remains of black flames, and remains of red-gold spiritual power.

00:00–00:02
Short cuts between a close-up of fingertips, eyes, and palm. The camera circles slightly around the fingertips, crash-zooms into the golden spiritual light on the palm, and switches to a close-up of Akane's face. Akane quietly forms a seal. Expression is focused. Ends of hair and sleeves sway slightly.

00:02–00:04
The camera spirals upward rapidly around Akane, transitioning to a top-down view to show the entire battlefield. The central summoning circle expands rapidly. Golden light erupts from the cracked stone floor. Floating rubble rotates. Clearly show the torii gate in the back right, the fallen pillar in the front left, the altar ruins in the back, and the thunderclouds. Akane pushes her spiritual power into the ground.

00:04–00:06
The camera dives rapidly into the center of the summoning circle, half-orbiting while grazing the vortex of spiritual power. Strongly show the beast's eyes, fangs, mane, and front legs in sequence. The beast's head, front legs, and entire body manifest in order. Red-gold and ink-black spiritual power swirl violently.
Akane's dialogue:
'O raging god, manifest from the depths of the earth! Respond to my call, Divine Beast!'

00:06–00:08
The camera grazes the beast's chest and retreats, orbiting widely while keeping Akane and the beast in the same frame. Finally shows a full-body pose of the beast. The beast roars and stamps the ground. Akane's outfit completion of change to priest-style combat gear. Hair and clothes sway violently from the wind pressure.

00:08–00:10
The darkness in front splits. The camera moves at high speed close to the ground into the darkness, enters the feet of the Ghost King, looks up along the greatsword to show the horns and eyes, and finally looks up at the entire body. The Ghost King slowly walks out dragging the greatsword. The stone floor cracks, and black flames and red sparks scatter.
Ghost King's dialogue:
'Do you think you can stop me with that level of power?'

00:10–00:12
High-speed pan from Akane's profile to the beast. The camera tracks alongside the beast, circling in front halfway to show the charge approaching from the front. Akane holds the spirit blade and points forward. The beast crouches low and charges all at once toward the Ghost King.
Akane's dialogue:
'Go!'

00:12–00:14
The camera circles to the Ghost King's side, showing the greatsword swing from a super low angle. At the moment of impact, insert a strong impact like a freeze frame for a moment, then move violently. The Ghost King swings down a hit clad in black flames. The beast is hit head-on and blown away. The stone floor shatters, rubble scatters, and a strong shockwave of black flames spreads.
Ghost King's dialogue:
'Too slow.'

00:14–00:16
The camera is washed away significantly following the blown-away beast, showing the shattered floor and dancing rubble while turning back to Akane. Akane gasps at the impact and almost kneels for a moment. Clear hit marks remain on the beast's shoulder and flank. These marks will remain in subsequent cuts.

00:16–00:18
The camera rapidly approaches Akane's face, capturing the determination in her eyes, then drops to the seal on her chest. Akane grits her teeth and recovers, forming a seal again. The weakening summoning circle lights up again, and a red-gold spiritual line extends from Akane to the beast. Show the collapsed torii and altar ruins again.
Akane's dialogue:
'It's not over yet... Stand up, Divine Beast! Let's fight together!'

00:18–00:21
The camera pushes in a straight line from behind Akane to the beast, then orbits nearly 360 degrees around the beast. The beast roars and stands up while keeping the hit marks. The red-gold glow of the whole body intensifies, and the mane stands up as if burning. Akane also readies her spirit blade. Strongly show the resonance between Akane and the beast.
Akane's dialogue:
'Crush that arrogance to pieces!'

00:21–00:23
The camera orbits the side of the Ghost King at high speed while following the beast's lateral movement. The beast circles to the side of the Ghost King at high speed
```
